}

{
  "function": "async () => {\n    const deadline=Date.now()+60000;\n    while(Date.now()<deadline) {\n      const t=window.vm?.runtime?.targets.find(t=>t.getName()==='Harness Robot');\n      if(t && document.querySelector('canvas')?.width>0) return {name:t.getName(),costumes:t.getCostumes().length,blocks:Object.values(t.blocks._blocks).filter(b=>!b.shadow).length,runtimeBlocksIncludingShadows:Object.keys(t.blocks._blocks).length};\n      await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,100));\n    }\n    throw Error('Harness Robot did not load');\n  }"
}

### Tool call: browser_evaluate
- Args
```json
{
  "function": "async () => {\n    const expectedSteps=null;\n    const deadline=Date.now()+3000;\n    while(expectedSteps!==null && vm.runtime.getTargetForStage().variables.var_steps.value!==expectedSteps && Date.now()<deadline) {\n      await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,16));\n    }\n    const t=vm.runtime.targets.find(t=>t.getName()==='Harness Robot');\n    const stage=vm.runtime.getTargetForStage();\n    return {x:t.x,y:t.y,visible:t.visible,costume:t.currentCostume,variables:Object.fromEntries(Object.values(stage.variables).map(v=>[v.name,v.value])),threads:vm.runtime.threads.length,pageErrors:window.__harnessErrors||[]};\n  }"
}
```

### Tool call: browser_evaluate
- Args
```json
{
  "function": "async () => {\n    const deadline=Date.now()+60000;\n    while(Date.now()<deadline) {\n      const t=window.vm?.runtime?.targets.find(t=>t.getName()==='Harness Robot');\n      if(t && document.querySelector('canvas')?.width>0) return {name:t.getName(),costumes:t.getCostumes().length,blocks:Object.values(t.blocks._blocks).filter(b=>!b.shadow).length,runtimeBlocksIncludingShadows:Object.keys(t.blocks._blocks).length};\n      await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,100));\n    }\n    throw Error('Harness Robot did not load');\n  }"
}

### Tool call: browser_evaluate
- Args
```json
{
  "function": "async () => {\n    const expectedSteps=null;\n    const deadline=Date.now()+3000;\n    while(expectedSteps!==null && vm.runtime.getTargetForStage().variables.var_steps.value!==expectedSteps && Date.now()<deadline) {\n      await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,16));\n    }\n    const t=vm.runtime.targets.find(t=>t.getName()==='Harness Robot');\n    const stage=vm.runtime.getTargetForStage();\n    return {x:t.x,y:t.y,visible:t.visible,costume:t.currentCostume,variables:Object.fromEntries(Object.values(stage.variables).map(v=>[v.name,v.value])),threads:vm.runtime.threads.length,pageErrors:window.__harnessErrors||[]};\n  }"
}
```

```json

  "result": "{\n  \"x\": 0,\n  \"y\": 0,\n  \"visible\": true,\n  \"costume\": 0,\n  \"variables\": {\n    \"steps\": 0,\n    \"clicks\": 0,\n    \"ready\": 1\n  },\n  \"threads\": 1,\n  \"pageErrors\": []\n}",
  "code": "await page.evaluate('async () => {\\n    const expectedSteps=null;\\n    const deadline=Date.now()+3000;\\n    while(expectedSteps!==null && vm.runtime.getTargetForStage().variables.var_steps.value!==expectedSteps && Date.now()<deadline) {\\n      await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,16));\\n    }\\n    const t=vm.runtime.targets.find(t=>t.getName()===\\'Harness Robot\\');\\n    const stage=vm.runtime.getTargetForStage();\\n    return {x:t.x,y:t.y,visible:t.visible,costume:t.currentCostume,variables:Object.fromEntries(Object.values(stage.variables).map(v=>[v.name,v.value])),threads:vm.runtime.threads.length,pageErrors:window.__harnessErrors||[]};\\n  }');"
}

}

{
  "function": "async () => {\n    const expectedSteps=1;\n    const deadline=Date.now()+3000;\n    while(expectedSteps!==null && vm.runtime.getTargetForStage().variables.var_steps.value!==expectedSteps && Date.now()<deadline) {\n      await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,16));\n    }\n    const t=vm.runtime.targets.find(t=>t.getName()==='Harness Robot');\n    const stage=vm.runtime.getTargetForStage();\n    return {x:t.x,y:t.y,visible:t.visible,costume:t.currentCostume,variables:Object.fromEntries(Object.values(stage.variables).map(v=>[v.name,v.value])),threads:vm.runtime.threads.length,pageErrors:window.__harnessErrors||[]};\n  }"
}
```

}

{
  "function": "async () => {\n    const expectedSteps=1;\n    const deadline=Date.now()+3000;\n    while(expectedSteps!==null && vm.runtime.getTargetForStage().variables.var_steps.value!==expectedSteps && Date.now()<deadline) {\n      await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,16));\n    }\n    const t=vm.runtime.targets.find(t=>t.getName()==='Harness Robot');\n    const stage=vm.runtime.getTargetForStage();\n    return {x:t.x,y:t.y,visible:t.visible,costume:t.currentCostume,variables:Object.fromEntries(Object.values(stage.variables).map(v=>[v.name,v.value])),threads:vm.runtime.threads.length,pageErrors:window.__harnessErrors||[]};\n  }"
}
```
